## 复习 · 第 6 组

### 开场 · 本组手工件

**⚠️ 本组 9 题 —— #0349 第二次调出**
```
pick 又把它排在队首（"最久没测优先"，它 0/0 且上次 08-27）。**教练第二次手工调出**，理由不变且**尚未解除**：
　今天组3 的 #0185 已经让她自发写出 `Out of desperation` —— 同一个 out of ＋ 抽象名词的形状。
　本条题面按词表型规矩把两个块（out of curiosity ／ out of necessity）**整块给出**，
　她要做的只剩"放进句子" ⇒ 同日再测**读数近乎为零**。
⇒ 今天余下各组一律继续调出；**明天（新的一天）它会自然排到队首**，那时读数才干净。
⛔ 不硬凑 10 题（她 09-01 定「10 本来就是一个数字而已」）。
```

**手工件① 语言事实核查**

| 条目 | 目标形状（核死） | 结论 |
|---|---|---|
| #0326 | `The **effect** of this decision **on** the industry …` ／ `bearing its **consequences**` | effect **on** ✔ ｜ consequences（后果，常复数）✔ 两个成员配的介词不同 |
| #0108 | `is **strictly** enforced` ／ `**usually** have to wait` | 方式副词两处都行（strictly enforced ✔）· 频率副词必须进中位（usually have to ✔）|
| #0355 | `the **statutory** retirement age` ／ `a **mandatory** annual health check` | statutory ＝ 写在法律里的 ✔ mandatory ＝ 被强制要求的 ✔ |
| #0356 | `**rich in** mineral resources` ／ `highly **experienced**` | rich **in** ＋ 不可数 ✔ ｜ 说人用 experienced（⛔ 不说 rich in experience 那么绕）✔ |
| #0357 | `was **piloted** in three cities for two years` ／ `**rolled out** nationwide` | pilot 作动词 ✔ trial ✔ roll out ✔ 三个是一整套 |
| #0358 | `paid **as a lump sum**` ／ `submitted **all at once**` ／ `⛔ not **in instalments**` | lump sum ＝ 一笔钱（⛔ 不是"一次做完"）· all at once ＝ 一次做完 ✔ |
| #0359 | `is **beneficial to／for** children's concentration` ／ `can **facilitate** approval` | beneficial **to／for** ＋ 名词 ✔ ｜ facilitate 直接带宾语（⛔ 不接 to do）✔ |
| #0360 | `the **aroma** of fresh bread` ／ `an unpleasant **smell**` | aroma ＝ 褒义香味（食物、咖啡）✔ smell ＝ 中性偏负、可好可坏 ✔ |
| #0361 | `**transnational** crime` ／ `**cross-border** money flows` | transnational ＝ 跨越国界的（现象、犯罪、组织）✔ cross-border ＝ 跨境的（流动、交易）✔ |

**手工件② 自译落点 ＋ 绕开测试 ⇒ 提示**

| 条目 | 绕开测试 | ⇒ 提示 |
|---|---|---|
| #0326 | 词表型 ⇒ §6「一律给词」 | 给 **effect** ／ **consequences**，**介词留给她** |
| #0108 | 翻得出（把副词全塞句末也读得通）⇒ 可绕开，但**位置**正是考点，给词等于给答案 | **零提示**，靠中文点名"两个副词各自放对位置" |
| #0355 #0356 #0357 #0358 | 四条都是词表型 ⇒ 给词；且**存档题面是 08-27 建号当天写好、从未用过的** | 存档题面原样发，括号限定逐字保留 |
| #0359 | 词表型 ⇒ 给词，**⛔ 介词与后面的形式不给**（那是这条真正会出事的地方，08-30 原句括号就是这么写的）| 给 **beneficial** ／ **facilitate** |
| #0360 | 词表型 ⇒ 给词 | 给 **aroma** ／ **smell**（好闻／难闻各用各的）|
| #0361 | 词表型 ⇒ 给词 | 给 **transnational** ／ **cross-border** |

**手工件③ 换触发点**
```
#0326  08-29 用「这项改革对小企业的冲击比谁都大」（测 impact on）⇒ 今天换决定／行业，
       改测 **effect ＋ consequences** 两个没测过的成员，介词仍不给
#0108  08-30 用「no longer ＋ often」⇒ 今天换新规执行／申诉等待，改测 **方式副词 ＋ 频率副词** 的对照
#0359  08-30 测的是 conducive ／ contribute ／ help（成员账记着）⇒ 今天换 **beneficial ＋ facilitate**
#0360  08-30 测的是 fumes ／ give off ／ pungent ⇒ 今天换 **aroma ＋ smell**（褒贬对照那一层没测过）
#0361  08-30 测的是 multinational ／ overseas ／ cross-border ⇒ 今天换 **transnational**（唯一没测过的）
       ＋ cross-border 配对；场景从"跨国公司/海外市场"换到"跨国犯罪/跨境资金"
#0355 #0356 #0357 #0358  —— 四条的存档题面都是 **08-27 建号当天写好、至今没用过的** ⇒ 原样发
```

**手工件④ 组内排布核查**
```
⚠️ pick 未报组内冲突（本组九条分属 F01／F06／F08 三族，语义场彼此不搭）。
⚠️ 教练自查：
   ① **#0349 第二次调出**（见上），跨组撞车未解除
   ② #0355（法定／强制）与 #0357（试点／铺开）都带"政策"味道，但一个考**形容词选词**、
      一个考**三个动词块**，中文零关键词重叠 ⇒ ⛔ 不调组
   ③ #0356 的括号里有一句 `⛔ 后半不许再用 rich in` —— 这是 §6 禁的「别用 Y」形式，
      但它同时给了正向替代（`后半用 experienced`）⇒ 按 §6「原括号每一个限定逐字带过去」保留原样，
      **留痕待议**：这条括号下次重写时应改成纯正向（只写"后半用 experienced"）。
```

### 题面（发给她的逐字）
```
1. 这项决定对整个行业的影响要好几年才看得出来，而它带来的后果已经有人在承担了。
   （★ 两处分别用 effect 和 consequence 一族里的词，注意各自配哪个介词）

2. 这项新规执行得很严，而这类申诉通常要等上好几周。（★ "很严"和"通常"两个副词，各自放对位置）

3. 法定退休年龄是写在法律里的，而每年一次的体检则是公司强制要求的。
   （★ 两处分别用 statutory ／ mandatory）

4. 这个地区矿产资源丰富，而当地的老技工也都经验老到。
   （★ 前半用 rich in，后半用 experienced，⛔ 后半不许再用 rich in）

5. 这项政策先在三个城市试点了两年，效果不错才在全国铺开。
   （★ 三处分别用 pilot ／ trial ／ roll out）

6. 这笔补助是一次性发放的，但申请材料必须一次交齐，不能分几次补。
   （★ 三处分别用 as a lump sum ／ all at once ／ in instalments）

7. 早睡对孩子的注意力有好处，而一套清晰的流程能让审批快得多。
   （★ 两处分别用 beneficial ／ facilitate —— ⛔ 介词与后面的形式不给）

8. 厨房里飘出一股新烤面包的香味，而垃圾桶那边有股难闻的味儿。
   （★ 好闻的那股用 aroma，难闻的那股用 smell）

9. 打击跨国犯罪需要多国合作，而这类案件往往涉及跨境的资金流动。
   （★ 两处分别用 transnational ／ cross-border）
```

⏸ 第二次调出：#0349（跨组撞车未解除，明天自然排队首）

### 她的答案（逐字抄，一个字不改）
```
1. the effect of this decision on the overall industry will be not visible for several years , whereas its consequences are already being borne(被动的正在进行时，也在主动使用的范围之外，新建一个条目) by some
2. This new regulation is strictly enforced, while appeals of this kind typically(这个副词确实很难想到) take several weeks.
3. The statutory retirement age is written into law 写进法律需要背), whilee the annual physical examination is mandatory by company policy（by 后面不加冠词和复数可以记下)
4. This region is rich in mineral(这个词背一下) resources, and local senior technicians are equally expericed.
5. This policy had first been piloted in three different cities for a two-year period, and only after showing good results was it rolled out across the nation
6. This subsidy is paid as a lump sum, but the application materials must be submiited all at once rather than in instalments.
7. Going to bed early is beneficial to children's attention, whereas a clear process can facilitate the approval procedure.
8. The amora of freshly baked bread drifts out of (这个词组需要学一下） the kitchen , whereas there is an unpleasant smell (用 odour 是不是更好) coming from the trash can.
9. Combating(这个词需要背下) transnational crime requires multi-nation opperation, whereas cases of this kind often involve cross-border financial flows.
```

### 判定表 · 第 6 组

| # | 编号 | 判定 | 她写的（考点那一段） | 命中/漏掉的点 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | #0326 | ✅ | `the **effect** of this decision **on** …` ／ `its **consequences** are already being borne` | 两个成员都落地，**介词也各自对**：effect **on** ✔ · consequences 用复数 ✔（题面只给词、⛔ 不给介词）⇒ 连对 1 → **2 ⇒ 🎓**。⚠️ 同句 `will be not visible` 见顺带 |
| 2 | #0108 | ✅ **但当天记 ❌** | `is **strictly** enforced` ／ `**typically** take several weeks` | 本题考点完整命中：方式副词 `strictly` 夹在 be 与分词之间 ✔ · 频率副词 `typically` 进主语与动词之间 ✔。⛔ **但同一天第 1 题她写了 `will be not visible`（否定词没跟第一个助动词）—— 归的正是本条**（见顺带）⇒ 按 §3.2 同日口径「出现过 ❌ 就记 ❌」⇒ **连对 1 → 连错 1**。**本日第一条掉分的条目** |
| 3 | #0355 | ✅ | `The **statutory** retirement age` ／ `is **mandatory** by company policy` | 两个成员分工正确：statutory ＝ 写在法律里的 · mandatory ＝ 被强制要求的 ⇒ 连对 0 → **1**（本条 08-27 建号后**第一次被测**）|
| 4 | #0356 | ✅ | `is **rich in** mineral resources` ／ `technicians are equally **experienced**` | rich **in** ＋ 不可数 ✔；说人那一半没有再套 rich in（那是题面钉死的那一点）⇒ 连对 0 → **1**（**第一次被测**）|
| 5 | #0357 | ✅ | `had first been **piloted** in three different cities` ／ `was it **rolled out** across the nation` | 词表型要求 ≥2 个成员落地：pilot（动词）✔ roll out ✔ ⇒ 连对 0 → **1**（**第一次被测**）。⚠️ `trial` 那个成员今天没被行使，记进成员账 |
| 6 | #0358 | ✅ | `paid **as a lump sum**` ／ `submitted **all at once**` ／ `rather than **in instalments**` | **三个成员全落地**，而且 lump sum 用在"一笔钱"上、all at once 用在"一次做完"上 —— 那正是本条建号时串掉的两层 ⇒ **连错 1 → 连对 1** |
| 7 | #0359 | ✅ | `is **beneficial to** children's attention` ／ `can **facilitate** the approval procedure` | 两个成员的**后面接什么**都对：beneficial **to** ＋ 名词 ✔ · facilitate 直接带宾语（⛔ 没接 to do）✔ —— 题面故意不给介词与形式 ⇒ 连对 1 → **2 ⇒ 🎓** |
| 8 | #0360 | ✅ | `The **aroma** of freshly baked bread` ／ `an unpleasant **smell**` | 褒贬两侧各归各位：aroma 配新烤面包 ✔ smell 配垃圾桶 ✔ ⇒ 连对 1 → **2 ⇒ 🎓** |
| 9 | #0361 | ✅ | `**transnational** crime` ／ `**cross-border** financial flows` | 两个成员各修饰对的东西：transnational 配 crime（跨越国界的现象）· cross-border 配 flows（跨境流动）⇒ 连对 1 → **2 ⇒ 🎓** |

### 顺带判定 · 第 6 组

**她顺带用错的**

| 出处 | 写的 | 该是 | 归到哪 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `will **be not** visible` | `will **not be** visible` | **#0108** —— 否定词必须跟在**第一个助动词**后面。★ 与 08-29 那次 `can **be no longer** verified` 是**同一格**（那次 ❌，08-30 守住，今天又塌）⇒ 归入本条，⛔ 不新建 |

**她顺带用对的 / 顺带 ⚠️**

| 出处 | 项 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | `are already being borne` | **被动进行时用对了**（be being ＋ 过去分词），而且 bear 的过去分词是 borne ✔ ⇒ 她当场点名要建 ⇒ #0400 |
| 3 | `is written into law` | 「写进法律」的标准块 ✔ ⇒ 她点名要背 ⇒ #0401 |
| 3 | ⚠️ `mandatory **by** company policy` | 规章类固定状语用 **under**：`under company policy` ／ `under the rules`；`by` 留给 `by law`。她问的"by 后面不加冠词和复数"⇒ 这一族**全是零冠词单数**，一并写进 #0401 |
| 4 | ⚠️ `are **equally** experienced` | equally ＝ "同样地（和某物一样）"，需要一个可比对象；中文「也都」是"同样也很" ⇒ `are also highly experienced` |
| 5 | `only after showing good results **was it** rolled out` | ★★ **only 提前 ＋ 主谓倒装，她自己做出来了** —— 这正是同一天组5 第 5 题 #0126 缺的那一手（`only in a few industries **are they**…`）⇒ **形状在她范围内，缺的是"看见中文里的只有"** |
| 5 | ⚠️ `had first been piloted` | 过去完成时不必要（没有更早的参照点），`was first piloted` 即可；不判错 —— 与后半 `only after … was it rolled out` 的先后关系读得通 |
| 7 | ⚠️ `a clear **process** … the approval **procedure**` | process 与 procedure 同义，同一句里撞了（#0221 的形状）⇒ `a clear procedure can facilitate approval` |
| 8 | `drifts out of` | 「（气味）飘出」的自然动词 ✔ ⇒ 她点名要学 ⇒ **扩写 #0360**（那条本来就管气味一族的动词 give off／emit），⛔ 不新建 |
| 8 | 她问「用 odour 是不是更好」 | **两个都行**：`smell` 中性偏口语、好坏都能配（要靠形容词定调）；`odour` 更书面、**本身已偏负**，所以常配 pungent／unpleasant（08-30 她写的 `pungent odour` 就是这一档）。这句里 `an unpleasant smell` 完全成立 ⇒ ⛔ 不换（§4.3.1②）|
| 9 | ⚠️ `multi-nation opperation` | 「多国合作」的标准说法是 **international cooperation** ／ `cooperation between countries`；`multi-nation` 虽有母语者用例（a multi-nation force）但配 cooperation 很少见 ⇒ 进更好版。`opperation` 见脚注 |
| 9 | `Combating` | 「打击（犯罪）」的标准动词 ✔ ⇒ 她点名要背 ⇒ #0403 |

**R 项扫描**

| R | 结果 | 证据 |
|---|---|---|
| R1 动词词尾 | ✅ 零命中 | `enforced` `written` `piloted` `rolled out` `paid` `submitted` `borne` 全对（borne 是 bear 的标准过去分词）|
| R2 名词与主谓 -s | ✅ 零命中 | `appeals … take` ✔ `technicians are` ✔ `materials must be` ✔ `cases … involve` ✔ |
| R3 固定块小零件 | ✅ 零命中 | `as a lump sum` ✔ `all at once` ✔ `in instalments` ✔ `beneficial to` ✔ `rich in` ✔ |
| R4 副词 -ly | ✅ 零命中 | `strictly` `typically` `freshly` `equally` `already` |
| R5 冠词 | ✅ 零命中 | `a lump sum` ✔ `a two-year period` ✔ `the approval procedure` ✔ `by company policy`（固定状语零冠词）✔ |

### bc 三版对照块 · 第 6 组
```
#1
  原句　　　 the effect of this decision on the overall industry will be not visible for several years , whereas its consequences are already being borne by some
  最小修改　 The effect of this decision on the overall industry will not be visible for several years, whereas its consequences are already being borne by some.
  └ 改了什么 `will be not` → `will not be`：否定词必须跟在**第一个助动词**后面（归 #0108；与 08-29 的 `can be no longer verified` 同一格）。句首大小写与逗号前的空格是排版，豁免。
  更好版　　 〔没有更好的版本〕
  └ 为什么好 `effect … on` 的介词、`consequences` 的复数、`are already being borne` 的被动进行时都到位，没有可换的。

#2
  原句　　　 This new regulation is strictly enforced, while appeals of this kind typically take several weeks.
  最小修改　 〔未改〕
  └ 改了什么 〔未改〕。两个副词各自放对：方式副词夹在 be 与分词之间、频率副词进主语与动词之间。
  更好版　　 〔没有更好的版本〕
  └ 为什么好 `appeals of this kind` 这个后置限定也比 `this kind of appeals` 顺。

#3
  原句　　　 The statutory retirement age is written into law, whilee the annual physical examination is mandatory by company policy
  最小修改　 〔未改〕
  └ 改了什么 〔未改〕。statutory ／ mandatory 两个成员分工正确（`whilee` 是手滑，见脚注）。
  更好版　　 The statutory retirement age is written into law, while the annual physical examination is mandatory under company policy.
  └ 为什么好 规章类的固定状语用 **under**（under company policy ／ under the rules）；`by` 留给 `by law`。这一族全部零冠词、名词用单数 —— 正是她问的那一点，已写进 #0401。
  〔脚注〕手滑豁免：`whilee` → `while`（不是真词）· 括号里那句是她的批注，不算句子成分。

#4
  原句　　　 This region is rich in mineral resources, and local senior technicians are equally expericed.
  最小修改　 〔未改〕
  └ 改了什么 〔未改〕。`rich in` ＋ 不可数名词 ✔；说人那一半没有再套 rich in，题面钉死的那一点守住了。
  更好版　　 This region is rich in mineral resources, and local senior technicians are also highly experienced.
  └ 为什么好 `equally` ＝ "同样地（和某个东西一样）"，要有一个可比对象；中文「也都经验老到」是"同样也很"，用 `also highly` 才对得上。
  〔脚注〕手滑豁免：`expericed` → `experienced`（不是真词、上下文只有一种读法）。

#5
  原句　　　 This policy had first been piloted in three different cities for a two-year period, and only after showing good results was it rolled out across the nation
  最小修改　 〔未改〕
  └ 改了什么 〔未改〕。pilot ／ roll out 两个成员落地（句末缺句号排版豁免）。
  更好版　　 This policy was first piloted in three cities for two years, and only after it had shown good results was it rolled out across the country.
  └ 为什么好 三处：① `had first been piloted` → `was first piloted` —— 没有更早的参照点，过去完成时是多余的一层；先后关系由后半句的 `only after` 扛着。② `for a two-year period` → `for two years`（`a … period` 是把两个词的意思说成六个词的名词块）。③ `only after showing` → `only after it had shown` —— 分词的逻辑主语要与主句主语一致，这里主句主语是 it（policy），showing 的动作也是 policy 发出的，读得通；但换成从句后"效果不错"是**谁的**就不再靠推断。★★ ⛔ **倒装那一手一个字不动** —— `only after … was it rolled out` 是本组最亮的一处。

#6
  原句　　　 This subsidy is paid as a lump sum, but the application materials must be submiited all at once rather than in instalments.
  最小修改　 〔未改〕
  └ 改了什么 〔未改〕。三个成员全落地，且 lump sum（一笔钱）与 all at once（一次做完）没有串台 —— 那正是本条建号时错的两层。
  更好版　　 〔没有更好的版本〕
  └ 为什么好 `rather than in instalments` 把"不能分几次补"接得干净，没有可换的。
  〔脚注〕手滑豁免：`submiited` → `submitted`。

#7
  原句　　　 Going to bed early is beneficial to children's attention, whereas a clear process can facilitate the approval procedure.
  最小修改　 〔未改〕
  └ 改了什么 〔未改〕。beneficial **to** ＋ 名词 ✔ · facilitate 直接带宾语 ✔ —— 题面故意不给介词与形式，两处都是她自己定的。
  更好版　　 Going to bed early is beneficial to children's concentration, whereas a clear procedure can facilitate approval.
  └ 为什么好 两处：① `attention` → `concentration` —— 「注意力（能集中多久）」英语默认 concentration；attention 更偏"注意到某事"。② `a clear process … the approval procedure` —— process 与 procedure 是同义词，同一句里撞了（#0221 的形状）⇒ 留一个，宾语直接用 approval。

#8
  原句　　　 The amora of freshly baked bread drifts out of the kitchen , whereas there is an unpleasant smell coming from the trash can.
  最小修改　 〔未改〕
  └ 改了什么 〔未改〕。aroma（褒）与 smell（这里配 unpleasant）各归各位，考点命中。
  更好版　　 〔没有更好的版本〕
  └ 为什么好 `drifts out of` 是「（气味）飘出」的自然动词，`an unpleasant smell coming from…` 也标准。⛔ 不换成 odour：两个都行 —— smell 中性偏口语、靠形容词定调；odour 更书面且本身偏负（所以常配 pungent／unpleasant）。她这句本身正确 ⇒ §4.3.1② 不许换。
  〔脚注〕手滑豁免：`amora` → `aroma`（不是真词）· 逗号前的空格是排版。

#9
  原句　　　 Combating transnational crime requires multi-nation opperation, whereas cases of this kind often involve cross-border financial flows.
  最小修改　 Combating transnational crime requires international cooperation, whereas cases of this kind often involve cross-border financial flows.
  └ 改了什么 `multi-nation opperation` → `international cooperation`：① 「多国合作」的标准说法是 international cooperation ／ cooperation between countries；`multi-nation` 虽有母语者用例（a multi-nation force），配 cooperation 极少见。② `opperation` 不是真词 —— 中文写的是"合作"，唯一说得通的是 cooperation，按 §3.2 属手滑；但因为整个块要换，这一处并进最小修改。
  更好版　　 〔没有更好的版本〕
  └ 为什么好 transnational ／ cross-border 各修饰对的东西，`financial flows` 也准，改完就到位了。
```

### 题面（发给她的逐字）
```
1. 这两个项目是并行推进的；但同时，两边的人手都很紧。
   （★ 两处分别用 in parallel ／ at the same time）

2. 这套行业标准被当成了定价的基准，而那场事故则成了整场讨论的焦点。
   （★ 两处分别用 benchmark ／ focal point，注意各自配哪个介词）

3. 不少公共服务都在涨价，包括水电和公交；有些城市，比如东部那几个，涨得尤其快。
   （★ 两处分别用 including ／ such as）

4. 这家店走的是极简的风格，而整体审美这几年一直在变；这个趋势短期内不会停。
   （★ 三处分别用 style ／ aesthetic ／ trend）

5. 这条谣言在家长群里传得飞快，而学校的正式说明却迟迟没有下发。
   （★ 两处分别用 circulate ／ disseminate）

6. 人口老龄化推高了医疗支出，而减税刺激了消费；不过出口的增速正在放缓。
   （★ 三处分别用 drive ／ stimulate ／ decelerate）

7. 就成本而言这个方案更省；说到执行，难度就大了；从技术上看则完全可行。
   （★ 三处走三条不同的路：in … terms ／ when it comes to ／ 一个副词）

8. 语言障碍是新移民找工作时最大的一道坎，而学科之间的界限也在慢慢模糊。
   （★ 两处分别用 barrier ／ boundary）

9. 第二份报告的口径与第一份完全不同，因此两边的结论也是各自独立的。
   （★ 两处分别用 distinct ／ separate，注意 distinct 配哪个介词）
```

### 判定表 · 第 7 组

| # | 编号 | 判定 | 她写的（考点那一段） | 命中/漏掉的点 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | #0362 | ✅ | `**in parallel**; but **at the same time**, staffing is tight` | 两个成员都落地，而且 at the same time 用的是**转折义**、放在句首 —— 那正是今天点的那个成员 ⇒ 连对 1 → **2 ⇒ 🎓** |
| 2 | #0363 | ✅ | `served as **the benchmark for** pricing` ／ `became **the focal point of** the discussion` | 两个块的**介词各自对**：benchmark **for**（衡量什么）· focal point **of**（属于哪场讨论）⇒ 连对 1 → **2 ⇒ 🎓**。⚠️ 她自己说 for/of 是"蒙的" ⇒ 介词分工补进本条正文 |
| 3 | #0364 | ✅ | `**including** water, electricity, and public transport` ／ `such as those in the Ease` | 两个成员都落地，后面都接名词 ✔ ⇒ 连对 1 → **2 ⇒ 🎓**。📋 `are seeing price increases` 是很地道的说法 |
| 4 | #0365 | ✅ | `a minimalist **style**` ／ `the overall **aesthetic**` ／ `this **trend**` | 三个成员全落地，`the overall aesthetic` 作名词、带 the ✔ ⇒ 连对 1 → **2 ⇒ 🎓** |
| 5 | #0366 | ✅ | `**circulated** rapidly` ／ `has yet to be **disseminated**` | 两个成员分工正确：circulate 自己传开（不及物）· disseminate 被下发（及物·被动）⇒ 连对 1 → **2 ⇒ 🎓**。📋 `has yet to` 是今天组5 #0353 刚练的块，**同日自发复用** |
| 6 | #0367 | ✅ | `has **driven up**` ／ `have **stimulated**` ／ `is **decelerating**` | 三个成员全落地，方向也对（推高／刺激／放缓）⇒ 连对 1 → **2 ⇒ 🎓** |
| 7 | #0368 | ✅ | `**In costs terms**` ／ `**when it comes to** execution` ／ `**technically**` | **三条路各走一条**，没有重复用同一条 —— 那是本题钉死的要求 ⇒ 连对 1 → **2 ⇒ 🎓**。⚠️ `costs` 该是单数，见顺带 |
| 8 | #0370 | ✅ | `the primary **barrier for** new immigrants` ／ `the **boundaries between** subjects` | 两个成员都落地，`boundaries **between**` 的介词也对（题面按 08-30 的做法⛔ 不给介词）⇒ 连对 1 → **2 ⇒ 🎓** |
| 9 | #0371 | ✅ | `is completely **distinct from** that of the first` ／ `are also **separate**` | 两个成员都落地，而且她自己问的那个介词 **from 选对了**（不是 with）⇒ 连对 1 → **2 ⇒ 🎓**。📋 `that of the first` 是 #0064 的回指手法，**同日复用** |

### 顺带判定 · 第 7 组

**她顺带用错的**

| 出处 | 写的 | 该是 | 归到哪 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 7 | `In **costs** terms` | `In **cost** terms` | **新建 #0409** —— 名词坐到另一个名词前面当定语时，**一律用单数** |

**她顺带用对的 / 顺带 ⚠️**

| 出处 | 项 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | ⚠️ `are pushed forward` ／ `but at the same time` | ① 无施动者的被动读着空；`are being carried out in parallel` ／ `are running in parallel` 更顺。② `at the same time` 本身已带转折，前面再加 but 是同一层说两遍 ⇒ 都进更好版 |
| 2 | `served as` | 「充当／被当成」用得准（比 be regarded as 紧）—— 她当场点名要建 ⇒ #0404 |
| 3 | `are seeing price increases` | 「都在涨价」用 see ＋ 名词，是很地道的报刊写法 ✔ |
| 4 | `adopts` | 归 **#0374**（接受／吸收／采纳一族，adopt 是其成员）⇒ 📋 留痕，⛔ 不推进；⛔ 不新建 |
| 5 | `has yet to be disseminated` | **今天组5 #0353 刚练的 `have yet to`，同日在新句子里自发复用** ✔ |
| 5 | ⚠️ `parent chat groups` | 「家长群」更自然的是 `parents' group chats` ／ `parent WeChat groups` ⇒ 进更好版 |
| 6 | ⚠️ 全篇 `whereas` | 九句里她用了 **6 次 whereas**。⚠️ 但**这一半是我的账**：九个中文题面里六个写的是「而…」。⛔ 不判、不建条目；**作文里要换**（同一个连接词连着用两次以上就该换）——记进收尾观察 |
| 7 | ⚠️ `poses significant difficulty` | difficulty 在这个意思上通常用复数：`poses significant difficulties` ／ 更省：`is much harder` ⇒ 进更好版 |
| 8 | `boundaries **between** subjects` | 介词题面没给，她自己补对了 ✔ |
| 8 | ⚠️ `subjects` | 学术语境里"学科"更常用 `disciplines`；subjects 偏中小学科目 ⇒ 进更好版 |
| 9 | `that of the first` | **#0064 的回指手法**（今天组1 刚测过 `those of employees`），同日在新句子里自发复用 ✔ |
| 6 | ⚠️ `Population aging` | 美式拼法（英式 ageing）。⚠️ **今天第三处混用**：组2 `Transportation`、组4 `city center`、本组 `aging` ⇒ 作文里挑一套用到底 |

**R 项扫描**

| R | 结果 | 证据 |
|---|---|---|
| R1 动词词尾 | ✅ 零命中 | `has driven up` `have stimulated` `circulated` `is decelerating` `has been shifting` 全对 |
| R2 名词与主谓 -s | ✅ 零命中 | `services are seeing` ✔ `prices are rising` ✔ `boundaries are blurring` ✔ `conclusions are` ✔ |
| R3 固定块小零件 | ✅ 零命中 | `in parallel` ✔ `at the same time` ✔ `benchmark **for**` ✔ `focal point **of**` ✔ `distinct **from**` ✔ `boundaries **between**` ✔ —— **六个块的介词全对**，这是本组最干净的一项 |
| R4 副词 -ly | ✅ 零命中 | `rapidly` `particularly` `gradually` `technically` `entirely` |
| R5 冠词 | ✅ 零命中 | `the benchmark for` ✔ `a minimalist style` ✔ `the overall aesthetic` ✔ `the primary barrier` ✔ |

### bc 三版对照块 · 第 7 组
```
#1
  原句　　　 These two projects are pushed forward in parallel; but at the same time, staffing is tight on both sides.
  最小修改　 〔未改〕
  └ 改了什么 〔未改〕。in parallel ＋ at the same time（转折义、句首）两个成员都落地。
  更好版　　 These two projects are running in parallel; at the same time, staffing is tight on both sides.
  └ 为什么好 两处：① `are pushed forward` → `are running` —— 无施动者的被动读着空（是谁在推？），而"项目并行推进"英语默认用 run in parallel。② 去掉 `but` —— `at the same time` 本身已经带转折，两个转折标记叠在一起是同一层说两遍。

#2
  原句　　　 this industry standard served as the benchmark for pricing, whereas that accident became the focal point of the entire discussion.
  最小修改　 〔未改〕
  └ 改了什么 〔未改〕。两个块的介词都对（句首小写豁免）。
  更好版　　 〔没有更好的版本〕
  └ 为什么好 `served as` 比 `was regarded as` 紧（说的是它实际起了这个作用，不是"被人看作"）；两个介词也正好。

#3
  原句　　　 a good number of public services are seeing price increases, including water, electricity, and public transport; in some cities, such as those in the Ease, prices are rising particularly rapidly.
  最小修改　 〔未改〕
  └ 改了什么 〔未改〕。including ／ such as 后面都接名词，考点命中。
  更好版　　 〔没有更好的版本〕
  └ 为什么好 `are seeing price increases` 是报刊里最常见的说法，比 `are becoming more expensive` 还紧。
  〔脚注〕手滑豁免：`the Ease` → `the East`（不是真词、上下文只有一种读法）。

#4
  原句　　　 This shop adopts a minimalist style, whereas the overall aesthetic has been shifting in recent years; this trend will not stop in the short term.
  最小修改　 〔未改〕
  └ 改了什么 〔未改〕。style ／ aesthetic ／ trend 三个成员全落地。
  更好版　　 〔没有更好的版本〕
  └ 为什么好 `adopts a minimalist style` ／ `the overall aesthetic` ／ `in the short term` 三个块都自然。

#5
  原句　　　 This rumor circulated rapidly in parent chat groups, wheres the school's offical statement has yet to be disseminated.
  最小修改　 〔未改〕
  └ 改了什么 〔未改〕。circulate（自己传开）与 disseminate（被下发）分工正确。
  更好版　　 This rumour circulated rapidly in parents' group chats, whereas the school's official statement has yet to be disseminated.
  └ 为什么好 `parent chat groups` → `parents' group chats`：中文「家长群」的中心词是"群聊"，家长是所有者 ⇒ 所有格 ＋ group chats；她那个语序读成"家长式的聊天组"。（`rumour` 是英式拼法，与她全篇的英式选择一致。）
  〔脚注〕手滑豁免：`wheres` → `whereas` · `offical` → `official`。

#6
  原句　　　 Population aging has driven up healthcare spending, whereas tax reductions have stimulated comsumption; however, the growth of exports is decelerating.
  最小修改　 〔未改〕
  └ 改了什么 〔未改〕。三个成员全落地，方向也对。
  更好版　　 Population ageing has driven up healthcare spending, whereas tax cuts have stimulated consumption; export growth, however, is decelerating.
  └ 为什么好 三处：① `aging` → `ageing`（英式；她全篇其他地方走的是英式，⛔ 一篇里别混两套）② `tax reductions` → `tax cuts`（同义，但 tax cuts 是这个概念的固定说法）③ `the growth of exports` → `export growth`（复合名词比 of 结构紧，也避开一个 the）。
  〔脚注〕手滑豁免：`comsumption` → `consumption`。

#7
  原句　　　 In costs terms, this proposal is more economical; when it comes to execution, it poses significant difficulty; technically, however, it is entirely feasible
  最小修改　 In cost terms, this proposal is more economical; when it comes to execution, it poses significant difficulty; technically, however, it is entirely feasible.
  └ 改了什么 `costs` → `cost`：名词坐到另一个名词前面当定语时**一律用单数**（cost terms · car park · book shop）⇒ 新建 #0409。三条路各走一条的考点不受影响。
  更好版　　 In cost terms, this proposal is more economical; when it comes to implementation, it poses significant difficulties; technically, however, it is entirely feasible.
  └ 为什么好 两处：① `execution` → `implementation` —— 政策／方案落地英语默认 implementation；execution 更偏"执行一个动作／处决"。② `poses significant difficulty` → `difficulties` —— difficulty 表"困难之处"时可数、常复数。

#8
  原句　　　 language servers as the primary barrier for new immigrants finding jobs, whereas the boundaries between subjects are gradually blurring.
  最小修改　 〔未改〕
  └ 改了什么 〔未改〕。barrier ／ boundaries between 两个成员都落地，介词题面没给、她自己补对了。
  更好版　　 Language serves as the primary barrier for new immigrants looking for work, whereas the boundaries between disciplines are gradually blurring.
  └ 为什么好 两处：① `new immigrants finding jobs` → `looking for work` —— finding jobs 说的是"找到了"，中文是"找工作（这件事）"，look for work 才对得上。② `subjects` → `disciplines` —— 学术语境里"学科"用 disciplines；subjects 偏中小学科目。
  〔脚注〕手滑豁免：`servers` → `serves`（撞成了另一个真词，但按 §3.2「判据是是不是选词」——上下文里只有 serves 说得通 ⇒ 豁免）· 句首两处多余空格。

#9
  原句　　　 The scope of the second report is completely distinct from that of the first, so the conclusions of the two are also seperate.
  最小修改　 〔未改〕
  └ 改了什么 〔未改〕。distinct **from** ✔（她自己问的那个介词，选对了）· separate ✔ 两个成员都落地。
  更好版　　 〔没有更好的版本〕
  └ 为什么好 `that of the first` 这个回指（#0064 的手法）用得干净，`the conclusions of the two` 也清楚。
  〔脚注〕手滑豁免：`seperate` → `separate`（不是真词）。
```

pick 报候选池 56 条，但逐条看「上次」：
　44 条  上次 ＝ **今天**（今天建号的，或今天已经判过还在池的）⇒ 再测是同日重复，读数近乎为零
　 9 条  上次 08-31
　 2 条  上次 08-30
　 1 条  #0349（上次 08-27，今天已三次调出）
⇒ **今天真正还没测过的只有 12 条** ⇒ 本组出 10 条（#0051 调出、#0349 留到明天），复习部分到此为止。
⚠️ 这个数 `pick` 自己算不出来 —— 它按"最久没测优先"排序，但**同一天内已经测过的条目仍在池里**。
　 教练手工按日期拆了一遍才看清。⇒ 留痕待议：pick 是否该把"今天已判过"的排到最后。

**⚠️ #0051 调出（不是撞车，是它本身出不了单点题）**
```
它的中文触发点写着「写出一个名词块时先问自己」，条目标题里也写着
**「（无 floor —— 这是个检查动作不是表达）」** —— 这不是一句能翻译的中文，是一个**检查动作**。
按她 2026-08-31 定的「⛔ 检查型装备整类作废」（原话「我们追求是一遍过，检查只能检查出极少的问题」），
这条要么改写成"写的当下就生效"的形状，要么按 §2⑤ 挂 R 项。
⇒ **这是她那天挂起的待裁定项**（⛔ 教练不催、不替她决定）⇒ 今天调出，⛔ 不出题。
```

**手工件① 语言事实核查**

| 条目 | 目标形状（核死） | 结论 |
|---|---|---|
| #0372 | `a 12% **rise in** electricity prices` ／ `a rise **of** 12%` ／ `**from** 3,000 **to** 8,000` | rise **in** ＋ 涨的对象 · rise **of** ＋ 幅度 · from…to… ＋ 起止 ✔ 三个介词分工不同 |
| #0373 | `**thereby** slowing …` ／ `**which in turn** delayed …` ／ `**resulted in**` | thereby ＋ -ing（⛔ 不接从句）· which in turn ＋ 变位动词 · result in ＋ 名词 ✔ |
| #0049 | `A typical example is the Netherlands, **where** cycle lanes are denser than roads for cars.` | 一句话装下"举例 ＋ 说明"，用 where 挂定语从句 ✔ |
| #0073 | `If **a** student falls behind, the teacher gives **the** student extra help.` | a 引入 · the 回指 ✔ |
| #0221 | `has never opened another branch **since**` ／ `**to this day** it has only one outlet` | ⛔ never…since 已含"此后"；⛔ to this day ＋ still 是同一层说两遍 ✔ |
| #0248 | `Rents are now **much higher** than five years ago.` | much ＋ **比较级**（⛔ much high）✔ |
| #0375 | `**speculate in** the housing market` ／ `a **bubble**` ／ `**hoard**` | speculate **in** ＋ 市场 ✔ bubble 可数 ✔ hoard 及物 ✔ |
| #0376 | `**on the basis of** three years of data` ／ `**on the grounds of** incomplete documents` | on the basis of ＝ 依据（事实／数据）· on the grounds of ＝ 以…为理由（常配负面处置）✔ |
| #0377 | `**broken down into** a fibrous material` ／ `**converted into** sugars` ／ `**turned into** fuel pellets` | 三个都配 **into** ✔ |
| #0378 | `**Each of** these three routes **has** its own timetable` ／ `**One of** them **is** still on trial` | each／one of ＋ 复数 ⇒ 谓语**单数** ✔ |

### 题面（发给她的逐字）
```
1. 去年电价出现了百分之十二的涨幅，同期用电需求也有上升；到年底日均负荷已经从三千兆瓦涨到八千兆瓦。
   （★ 三处都用「涨幅／上升／从…到…」的**名词**说法 —— ⛔ 介词自己补）

2. 那家工厂停产切断了零件供应，从而拖慢了整条装配线，而这反过来又推迟了交付。
   （★ 三处分别用 thereby ／ which in turn ／ result in —— ⛔ 注意各自后面能接什么）

3. 一个典型的例子就是荷兰，那里的自行车道比机动车道还密。（★ 写成一句）

4. 如果哪个学生跟不上，老师就会给那个学生单独补课。（★ 第一次提到和回指要用不同的冠词）

5. 这家老店二十年前就停止扩张了，此后再没开过新店；到今天它仍然只有那一间铺面。
   （★ 两处：「此后再没开过」用一个词说完；「到今天…仍然」里不要出现两个表示"现在"的词）

6. 现在的租金比五年前高出很多。

7. 大量热钱涌进楼市炒房，房价很快脱离了实际价值；不少人还囤着房子不卖。
   （★ 三处分别用 speculate ／ bubble ／ hoard —— ⛔ 介词自己补）

8. 评审组是依据三年的实测数据做的决定；另有两份申请以材料不全为由被退了回来。
   （★ 两处分别用 on the basis of ／ on the grounds of）

9. 这些木屑先被拆解成一种纤维状的物质，随后又变成糖；剩下的边角料则被做成了燃料颗粒。
   （★ 三处分别用 break down ／ convert ／ turn —— ⛔ 介词自己补）

10. 这三条线路各自都有自己的时刻表；其中有一条现在还在试运行。
    （★ 两处分别用 Each of ／ One of 开头 —— ⛔ 动词形式自己定）
```

```
1.  Electricity prices saw an increase of 20% last year, parallel with a rise in electricity demand over the same period; the average daily load(需要背下) grew from 3,000 megawatts to 8,000;
2. The shutdown of that factory cut off the supply of parts, thereby slowing down the entire assembly(这个要背下) line, which in turn resulted in delayed deliveries.
3. A classic exmaple is the Neverlands, where the network of bicycle lanes is even denser than that of motor vehicle lanes.
4. If a student falls behind, the teacher will give the student extra tutoring.
5. That old shop stopped expanding twenty years ago and never opened another branch; To this day, it still operates just that single shop.
6. Today's rents are much higher than those of five years ago
7. A large amount of hot money flooded into the property market to speculate in real estate, causing house prices to bubble up far beyond their actual value; meanwhile, many people are hoarding houses rather than selling them.
8. The review panel(这个词组背一下) made its decision on the basis of three years of measured data; meanwhile, two other applications were returned on the grounds of insufficient documentation.
9. The wood chips are first broken down into a fabrious material,  which is subsequently converted into sugars, while the remaining scraps(这个词需要背一下) are turned into fuel pellets（这个词组需要背一下)
10. Each of these three lines has its own timetable; one of them is currently under trial operation. (在实验需要建一个条目练习下，另外 under XX，比如运营建设也需要一个条目)
```

### 判定表 · 第 8 组

| # | 编号 | 判定 | 她写的（考点那一段） | 命中/漏掉的点 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | #0372 | ✅ | `an increase **of** 20%` ／ `a rise **in** electricity demand` ／ `**from** 3,000 … **to** 8,000` | **三个介词全对**：of ＝ 幅度 · in ＝ 涨的对象 · from…to ＝ 起止 —— 题面⛔ 一个介词都没给 ⇒ 连对 1 → **2 ⇒ 🎓**。⚠️ 第三处她走的是**动词**（grew from…to）而不是名词说法，按 §3.2「点名是引导不是门」照样算对，但那一格的名词形状今天没被行使 |
| 2 | #0373 | ✅ | `**thereby** slowing down …, **which in turn** resulted in …` | 三个连接件后面接的东西全对：thereby ＋ -ing · which in turn ＋ 变位动词 · result in ＋ 名词 ⇒ 连对 1 → **2 ⇒ 🎓**。**整句一字未改** |
| 3 | #0049 | ✅ | `A classic example is the Netherlands, **where** …` | 举例写成**一句**（⛔ 没拆成两句、也没降级成 In the Netherlands, for example）⇒ 连对 1 → **2 ⇒ 🎓** |
| 4 | #0073 | ✅ | `If **a** student falls behind, the teacher will give **the** student …` | a 引入 · the 回指，两处冠词各归各位 —— 这正是她 08-31 自己推出来的那条规则 ⇒ 连对 1 → **2 ⇒ 🎓** |
| 5 | #0221 | ✅ | `**never opened another branch**`（此后再没开过，一个词说完）| 第一处考点命中：⛔ 没有写成 never…since then 那种叠加。⚠️ **第二处是我的题面出了问题**（见教练侧 ⑧）⇒ 连对 1 → **2 ⇒ 🎓** |
| 6 | #0248 | ✅ | `are **much higher** than …` | much ＋ **比较级**（⛔ 不是 much high）—— 08-31 从 🎓 降级正是栽在这一格 ⇒ **连错 1 → 连对 1** |
| 7 | #0375 | ✅ | `**speculate in** real estate` ／ `prices to **bubble up**` ／ `**hoarding** houses` | ⚠️ **教练原判 ❌，她当场推翻，已改判 ✅**（§3.2「教练判 ❌、她当场推翻 ⇒ 按 ✅ 记」，见教练侧 ⑨）。三个成员都落地：speculate **in** ✔ · bubble（动词形，金融报道里成立）✔ · hoard 及物 ✔ ⇒ **连错 1 → 连对 1**。⚠️ 名词形 `a property bubble` 这个成员今天**没被行使**，记进成员账 |
| 8 | #0376 | ✅ | `**on the basis of** three years of measured data` ／ `returned **on the grounds of** insufficient documentation` | 两个块分工正确：on the basis of ＝ 依据（事实／数据）· on the grounds of ＝ 以…为由（配负面处置）⇒ 连对 0 → **1**（08-31 建号后**第一次被测**）|
| 9 | #0377 | ✅ | `**broken down into**` ／ `**converted into**` ／ `**turned into**` | **三个成员全落地，而且三个 into 全对** —— 题面⛔ 没给介词 ⇒ 连对 0 → **1**（08-31 建号后第一次被测）|
| 10 | #0378 | ✅ | `**Each of** these three lines **has** …; **one of** them **is** …` | 两处都用**单数谓语** —— 这正是 08-31 她说"不会主动用"的那一格 ⇒ 连对 0 → **1**（第一次被测） |

|---|---|---|---|

| ~~7~~ | ~~`prices to **bubble up**`~~ | ~~`creating a property **bubble**`~~ | ⛔ **撤销** —— 她当场推翻，`bubble up` 用于价格在金融报道里成立（三条母语者反例见教练侧 ⑨）⇒ 本行作废，#0375 改判 ✅ |

| 3 | `that of motor vehicle lanes` | **今天第三处 `that/those of` 回指**（组1 #0064 · 组7 #0371 · 本组）—— #0064 的手法已经稳了 |
| 5 | ⚠️ `; To this day` | 分号后首字母不大写（§3.2 大小写豁免，但**作文里计入 GRA**）⇒ 当场点名 |
| 6 | `those of five years ago` | **今天第四处** that/those 回指 ✔ |
| 7 | `speculate **in** real estate` ／ `hoarding houses` | 两个成员的形式都对（speculate 配 in、hoard 及物）—— 错的只有 bubble 那一处 |

| 9 | `broken down into` ＋ `converted into` ＋ `turned into` | **三个 into 全对** —— 08-31 建号时她答"不会"的正是这一族 |
| 10 | `under trial operation` | `under ＋ 零冠词名词` 的形状她自己调出来了 ⇒ 她当场点名要建 ⇒ #0413 |

  原句　　　 Electricity prices saw an increase of 20% last year, parallel with a rise in electricity demand over the same period; the average daily load grew from 3,000 megawatts to 8,000;

  └ 为什么好 `saw an increase of` ／ `a rise in` ／ `grew from … to` 三种说法各占一处，没有重复，改完就到位了。

    但 `to this day … still` 是**完全地道的固定强调式**（`To this day I still don't understand.` ✔）。

　　 这类题面不给提示的格：三个 into 全对、Each of ＋ 单数谓语两处全对。

　　　　查重后 assembly line 属于该条的语义场 ⇒ 补进正文，⛔ 不新建

**⑨ #0375 改判 ❌ → ✅：教练在四问①上偷懒，她当场推翻（2026-09-01，§4.7）**
```
她的原话：「**我觉得这个没问题，最多把 house prices 改成 housing prices**」
原判：`causing house prices to **bubble up** far beyond their actual value` ⇒ ❌
　　　理由写的是「bubble 在这一族里是名词，bubble up 是'液体冒泡／问题浮上来'，⛔ 不用于价格」
新判：**✅**，连错 1 → 连对 1（§3.2：教练判 ❌、她当场推翻 ⇒ **按 ✅ 记并推进 streak**，
　　　不是"两边都不动"）

**判据哪里套宽了**（§4.7 要求写出来）
　教练脑子里只挂着 bubble up 的两个常见义（液体冒泡／问题浮上来），
　**没有真的去造母语者句子**就下了"不用于价格"的结论。造得出来，而且是金融报道的常见写法：
　　`Asset prices **bubbled up** as cheap credit flooded in.` ✔
　　`Prices **bubbled up** to unsustainable levels before the crash.` ✔
　　`Housing markets **bubbled up** across the region in the mid-2000s.` ✔
　⇒ 按 §0.8「说 X 不能这样用之前先造三个母语者反例，造得出就不许说」——**这话本就不该说**。
　⇒ 收紧后的判据（写进 #0375 正文）：**bubble 名词、动词两用**；
　　 名词 a property／housing bubble · the bubble bursts ｜ 动词 prices bubble up（报道体，成立）。
　　 ⛔ 以后判这一族只判"**词选对没对**"（speculate／bubble／hoard／overheat），
　　 ⛔ **不判她把哪个词用成了名词还是动词** —— 除非那个形式真的不存在。

**顺带一条也撤销**：`house prices` ⛔ 不必改成 `housing prices` ——
　house prices 是**英式标准说法**（英国国家统计局的官方口径就是 house prices），
　housing prices 偏美式。两个都对，她那个更稳（§4.3.1②：说不出用错在哪就不许换）。

⇒ 已同步四处：判定表第 7 行 · 顺带栏（划掉）· 三版对照块 #7 · 战报 ①②③④。
⚠️ **这是今天第二次同类犯规**：⑧（to this day ＋ still 的假要求）与本条，
　 **根子都是"判之前没跑四问① 造反例"**。⇒ 判据（本场立）：
　 **凡是要说"这个说法不对／不用于 X"，发出前必须在 session 里写下三条母语者反例的尝试结果**；
　 写不出三条 ⇒ 降档为 ⚠️，⛔ 不判 ❌。

限时   40 分钟　字数   270–290（scoring.md §3.4；这是练习设计，⛔ 交稿后不拿它当门槛）

本题历史（§7 题级风险，⛔ 不占靶子名额）
  2026-07-14 第 1 次 cold 限时 ＝ **6.5**（真源 bank.md 曲线行；TR 7.0 / CC 7.0 / LR 6.0 / GRA 6.0）
  那次两项低分全落在**语言**上，不是内容：
    · 单复数／主谓 **5 处**（a large amount of people · every T-shirts are · people benefits …）
      ⇒ 现在归 **R2**，⛔ 不占靶子名额，我判分时必扫
    · 拼写 **5 处**，其中 `go **rural**` → `go **viral**` 正是 **#0095**（拼成另一个真词）的形状
      ⇒ 这条今天刚在零提示下拿到 ✅（连对 1），**作文才是它真正的考场**

         ★ 本篇的具体形状：Body1 只讲 **how**（怎么形成的），Body2 只讲 **好/坏**，
           ⛔ 不许在 Body1 里提前评价好坏；两段段尾各自回扣 `global` / `positive／negative`
  靶子2  **介词**
         来源 08-25 三处失守全是动词自带的固定介词；08-27 与今天复习组都全对，但没清零

装备（≤1）
  交卷前把全文的介词逐个圈出来，凡是跟在动词后面的单独念一遍再定
  （今天复习组这一格 7/7 全对：to · onto · to · on · to · at · of —— 作文里能不能守住是另一回事）

### 回看 · T2-22 · 她的原文（逐句编号，逐字；§5 第 1 步按句号切，分号独立成句）

```
S1  Fashion in the past was mostly localized and regional;
S2  in recent years, trends have moved much faster towards global popularity.
S3  This essay will discuss the reasons behind this trend, as well as why I believe it is a positive development.
S4  This trend stems mainly from two factors: the globalization of supply chains and consumption, and the rapid development of social media.
S5  First of all, multinational clothing corporations deploy production lines globally and open retail chains around the world, making it possible for the same style of clothing to be released in different countries simultaneously.
S6  As a result, a single piece of clothing becomes a shared reference point recognized everywhere.
S7  Take Japan's Uniqlo as an example: people can purchase the exact same styles as those in Tokyo, driving a global trend towards a minimalist and modern Japanese fashion aesthetic.
S8  More importantly, social media eliminates barriers to the spread of information.
S9  Video created by individuals without significant influence can also go viral, sparking a wave of follow-up trends across the globe.
S10 These examples demonstrate that convenient and rapid access to identical information and materials has greatly accelerated the synchronization of global trends.
S11 I firmly believe this trend is highly positive development, both economically and culturally.
S12 From the economic perspective, the globalization of fashion trends can stimulate global consumption, thereby boosting economic growth and creating more jobs.
S13 A case in point is a jacket worn by a Korean idol at Kansai Airport that completely sold out across US online stores within just two days, after fan-edited clips of this outfit went viral on TikTok.
S14 From the cultural perspective, global fashion trends break down regional boundaries, allowing different countries to better understand one another through their respective clothing styles, while also introducing foreign elements into local culture.
S15 Japanese anime, for example, has become popular worldwide, leading to the rise in anime enthusiasts and cosplayers everywhere, which in turn makes local culture more diverse.
S16 In conclusion, the global popularity of fashion can be attributed to the globalization of supply chains and consumption, alongside the convenience of social media.
S17 This simultaneously stimulates global consumption and gives people opportunities to understand and embrace cultures from other places, which is precisely why I believe it is a highly positive development.
⇒ 共 17 句 · **372 词**（wc -w 实数，不估）
```

### 回看 · T2-22 · 最小修改版 · 全文

```
Fashion in the past was mostly localized and regional; in recent years, trends have moved much faster towards global popularity. This essay will discuss the reasons behind this trend, as well as why I believe it is a positive development.

This trend stems mainly from two factors: the globalization of supply chains and consumption, and the rapid development of social media. First of all, multinational clothing corporations deploy production lines globally and open retail chains around the world, making it possible for the same style of clothing to be released in different countries simultaneously. As a result, a single piece of clothing becomes a shared reference point recognized everywhere. Take Japan's Uniqlo as an example: people can purchase the exact same styles as those in Tokyo, driving a global trend towards a minimalist and modern Japanese fashion aesthetic. More importantly, social media eliminates barriers to the spread of information. Videos created by individuals without significant influence can also go viral, sparking a wave of follow-up trends across the globe. These examples demonstrate that convenient and rapid access to identical information and goods has greatly accelerated the synchronization of global trends.

I firmly believe this trend is a highly positive development, both economically and culturally. From the economic perspective, the globalization of fashion trends can stimulate global consumption, thereby boosting economic growth and creating more jobs. A case in point is a jacket worn by a Korean idol at Kansai Airport that completely sold out across US online stores within just two days, after fan-edited clips of this outfit went viral on TikTok. From the cultural perspective, global fashion trends break down regional boundaries, allowing different countries to better understand one another through their respective clothing styles, while also introducing foreign elements into local culture. Japanese anime, for example, has become popular worldwide, leading to the rise in anime enthusiasts and cosplayers everywhere, which in turn makes local culture more diverse.

In conclusion, the global popularity of fashion can be attributed to the globalization of supply chains and consumption, alongside the convenience of social media. This simultaneously stimulates global consumption and gives people opportunities to understand and embrace cultures from other places, which is precisely why I believe it is a highly positive development.
```
⇒ 改的 3 处：`Video`→`Videos`（S9）· `materials`→`goods`（S10）· 补 `a`（S11）

### 回看 · T2-22 · 更好版 · 全文

```
Fashion in the past was mostly localized and regional; in recent years, trends have moved much faster towards global popularity. This essay will discuss the reasons behind this trend, as well as why I believe it is a positive development.

This trend stems mainly from two factors: the globalization of supply chains and consumption, and the rapid development of social media. First of all, multinational clothing corporations deploy production lines globally and open retail chains around the world, making it possible for the same style of clothing to be released in different countries simultaneously. As a result, a single piece of clothing becomes a shared reference point recognized everywhere. Take Japan's Uniqlo as an example: people can purchase the exact same styles as those in Tokyo, driving a global trend towards a minimalist and modern Japanese fashion aesthetic. More importantly, social media eliminates barriers to the spread of information. Videos created by individuals without significant influence can also go viral, sparking a wave of imitators across the globe. These examples demonstrate that convenient and rapid access to identical information and goods has greatly accelerated the synchronization of global trends.

I firmly believe this trend is a highly positive development, both economically and culturally. From the economic perspective, the globalization of fashion trends can stimulate global consumption, thereby boosting economic growth and creating more jobs. A case in point is a jacket that completely sold out across US online stores within just two days, after fan-edited clips of a Korean idol wearing it at Kansai Airport went viral on TikTok. From the cultural perspective, global fashion trends break down regional boundaries, allowing different countries to better understand one another through their respective clothing styles, while also introducing foreign elements into local culture. Japanese anime, for example, has become popular worldwide, leading to a rise in the number of anime enthusiasts and cosplayers everywhere, which in turn makes local culture more diverse.

In conclusion, the global popularity of fashion can be attributed to the globalization of supply chains and consumption, alongside the convenience of social media. This simultaneously stimulates global consumption and gives people opportunities to understand and embrace cultures from other places, which is precisely why I believe it is a highly positive development.
```

### 回看 · T1-15 · 她的原文（逐句编号，逐字）
```
S1  The flow chart illustrates a multi-stage process of how to produce ethanol, as well as its subsequent use.
S2  Overall, it is a cyclical process consisting of several steps, beginning with the growth of plants and culminating in the release of carbon dioxide.
S3  The process commences when plants and trees utilize energy from sunlight and carbon dioxide to grow.
S4  Once these plants fully grown, they are harvested by machinery and are then delivered to undergo pre-processing, converting them into cellulose.
S5  In the following step, the cellulose is continuously processed to get sugars, which are subsequently mixed with microbes to produce the final product — ethanol.
S6  It can be used for fueling a variety of transports — cars, trunks and airplanes.
S7  These vehicles give off fumes and release carbon dioxide to the atmosphere, restarting the entire circle.

段落：P1 ＝ S1（引言）｜ P2 ＝ S2（overview）｜ P3 ＝ S3–S4 ｜ P4 ＝ S5–S7
```

### 回看 · T1-15 · 三版对照块（7 块，逐字）
```
#S1
  原句　　　 The flow chart illustrates a multi-stage process of how to produce ethanol, as well as its subsequent use.
  最小修改　 The flow chart illustrates a multi-stage process of producing ethanol, as well as its subsequent use.
  └ 改了什么 一处：`of how to produce` → `of producing`。`process` 的补语框架是 `the process of ＋ V-ing` 或 `the process by which … is produced`；`of how to do` 只挂在**说明类**名词后面（`an explanation of how to…` ✔ `a guide to how to…` ✔），⛔ 造不出 `a process of how to do` 的母语者句。★ `a multi-stage` 保留不动 —— 连字符前置修饰语 ＋ 单数，这一格写对了（⇒ #0369 ✅）。
  更好版　　 The flow chart illustrates the multi-stage process by which ethanol is produced, as well as its subsequent use.
  └ 为什么好 `of producing` → `by which … is produced`：全篇其余部分都是被动（are harvested／is processed／are mixed），开篇就用被动可以让整条链子的视角一致（⛔ 这不是"更高级"，是**与她自己的行文对齐**）。`a` → `the`：图上是**这一个**特定流程，不是"某一种"。内容一点没动：流程图／多阶段／生产乙醇／还有后续用途，四点全在。

#S2
  原句　　　 Overall, it is a cyclical process consisting of several steps, beginning with the growth of plants and culminating in the release of carbon dioxide.
  最小修改　 〔未改〕
  └ 改了什么 一个字没改。overview 的三件套齐了：循环（cyclical）· 阶段数（several steps）· 起点终点（beginning with … culminating in …）—— 这正是 2026-07-08 那篇的判词说她"一次就掌握"的那套，今天原样再现（⇒ TA ①②③ 三项全勾）。
  更好版　　 〔没有更好的版本〕
  └ 为什么好 ⚠️ 这一句有一个**内容层面**的自相矛盾：说了 `cyclical`（循环没有终点），又说 `culminating in the release of carbon dioxide`（给了一个终点）。⛔ 但这属于**她的内容**，按 §4.3.1 ① 更好版不许替她改内容层 —— 这一处已经在判分里作为 TA 第 ④ 项（漏"CO₂ 被新植物吸收"那一格）扣过了。⇒ 语言层面挑不出毛病，不动。下次写循环图，overview 的第二根柱子写"起点与回到起点"，⛔ 不写"终点"。

#S3
  原句　　　 The process commences when plants and trees utilize energy from sunlight and carbon dioxide to grow.
  最小修改　 〔未改〕
  └ 改了什么 一个字没改。无生命名词 `The process` 放主语位 ＋ 实义动词 `commences` 推动（⇒ #0149 ✅）；`utilize` 的主谓一致、`energy from … to grow` 的框架都对；图上 `Energy (sunlight + carbon dioxide)` 两个来源都送到了。
  更好版　　 〔没有更好的版本〕
  └ 为什么好 `commences` 是她自己选的正式动词、用得准；按 §4.3.1 ②，换成 begins 说不出她哪里用错或生硬 ⇒ ⛔ 不换。

#S4
  原句　　　 Once these plants fully grown, they are harvested by machinery and are then delivered to undergo pre-processing, converting them into cellulose.
  最小修改　 Once these plants are fully grown, they are harvested by machinery and are then delivered to undergo pre-processing, which converts them into cellulose.
  └ 改了什么 两处：① 补 `are`（`Once these plants **are** fully grown`）—— 原句缺谓语，是个片段；`fully grown` 是过去分词作表语，前面必须有 be。② `converting them into cellulose` → `**which converts** them into cellulose` —— 原句的分词悬垂了：分词的逻辑主语会被读成主句主语 `they`（＝ 植物），意思变成"植物把自己转化成纤维素"，可真正在做转化的是 pre-processing。改成 which 从句，把主语钉死在 pre-processing 上。
  更好版　　 Once these plants are fully grown, they are harvested by machinery and sent for pre-processing, which converts them into cellulose.
  └ 为什么好 一处：`are then delivered to undergo pre-processing` → `sent for pre-processing`。`deliver` 的默认宾语是"把货送到某个地方"，句子里必须有目的地；这里没有目的地，只有下一道工序 ⇒ 用 `send for ＋ 工序` 这个现成块（§4.3.1 ②b 生硬）。⛔ `converts` 保留 —— 她这个词本身正确，⛔ 不换成 breaks down。内容五点全在：长成后／机械收割／送去预处理／转成纤维素／主语仍是这批植物。

#S5
  原句　　　 In the following step, the cellulose is continuously processed to get sugars, which are subsequently mixed with microbes to produce the final product — ethanol.
  最小修改　 In the following step, the cellulose is further processed to yield sugars, which are subsequently mixed with microbes to produce the final product — ethanol.
  └ 改了什么 两处：① `continuously` → `**further**`。continuously ＝ **不间断地**（机器一刻不停），中文想说的是"**继续／进一步**加工" ⇒ 这是从中文字面找了一个英文词、而那个词在英语里指另一件事 ⇒ **本篇靶子2 的第一处失守**。② `to get sugars` → `to **yield** sugars`。get 是口语档，流程描述里用 yield／produce（yield 是你自己档案里用过的词，⛔ 不是我给你加的生词）。
  更好版　　 〔没有更好的版本〕
  └ 为什么好 最小修改之后这一句已经到位：`which are subsequently mixed` 的先行词、数、位置全对；`the final product — ethanol` 用破折号同位也干净。按 §4.3.1 ②，再动任何一个词都说不出"用错"或"生硬" ⇒ ⛔ 不动。

#S6
  原句　　　 It can be used for fueling a variety of transports — cars, trunks and airplanes.
  最小修改　 It can be used for fueling a variety of vehicles — cars, trucks and airplanes.
  └ 改了什么 两处：① `transports` → `**vehicles**`。transport 作"交通工具"是**不可数**，⛔ 不加 -s（同 advice／evidence 那一族）；而且你下一句自己就写了 `These vehicles` —— 词在手上，只是这一处走了中文"交通工具**们**"的字面 ⇒ **靶子2 的第二处失守**。② `trunks` → `**trucks**`。trunk ＝ 树干／后备箱，是**另一个真词**，意思整个变掉 ⇒ 按 §3.2，作文里形近词一律照记（⛔ 复习组的手滑豁免不适用于作文）。★ `airplanes` 是美式拼写，⛔ 不改（2026-07-08 你在同一道题上写的是 `airphanes`，今天拼对了）。
  更好版　　 〔没有更好的版本〕
  └ 为什么好 `be used for ＋ V-ing` 本身成立、`a variety of` 也是现成块；最小修改换掉两个词之后没有可挑的了。按 §4.3.1 ②，把 `used for fueling` 换成 `used to fuel` 说不出她哪里用错 ⇒ ⛔ 不换。

#S7
  原句　　　 These vehicles give off fumes and release carbon dioxide to the atmosphere, restarting the entire circle.
  最小修改　 These vehicles give off fumes and release carbon dioxide to the atmosphere, restarting the entire cycle.
  └ 改了什么 一处：`circle` → `**cycle**`。circle ＝ 圆圈（形状），cycle ＝ 循环（过程），是**另一个真词** ⇒ 意思变掉。★★ 这个词与 `trunks` 一起，**和 2026-07-08 你在同一道题上错的是同一对** —— 隔 54 天原样复发。⛔ `to the atmosphere` 我判**不算错**：`released to the atmosphere` 在技术散文里成立，造得出母语者句 ⇒ 按 §10 禁令 9 不许说它错，只进更好版。
  更好版　　 These vehicles give off exhaust fumes, releasing carbon dioxide into the atmosphere and restarting the entire cycle.
  └ 为什么好 两处：① `give off fumes **and** release carbon dioxide` → `give off exhaust fumes, **releasing** carbon dioxide` —— 原句两个并列谓语说的其实是**同一个排放动作**，并列读着像两件事；改成主从之后层次出来了：排废气 → 废气里的二氧化碳进入大气（§4.3.1 ②b 生硬）。② `to` → `into the atmosphere`：into 是这一族的默认搭配。内容五点全在：这些车辆／废气／二氧化碳／大气／重启整个循环。⚠️ 另记一笔：图上只标了 carbon dioxide、**没有 fumes** —— 这一层是你自己加的。它不算 T1 三禁（不是猜原因、不是下结论），但下次照图写更稳。
```

### 回看 · T1-15 · 最小修改版 · 全文
```
The flow chart illustrates a multi-stage process of producing ethanol, as well as its subsequent use.

Overall, it is a cyclical process consisting of several steps, beginning with the growth of plants and culminating in the release of carbon dioxide.

The process commences when plants and trees utilize energy from sunlight and carbon dioxide to grow. Once these plants are fully grown, they are harvested by machinery and are then delivered to undergo pre-processing, which converts them into cellulose.

In the following step, the cellulose is further processed to yield sugars, which are subsequently mixed with microbes to produce the final product — ethanol. It can be used for fueling a variety of vehicles — cars, trucks and airplanes. These vehicles give off fumes and release carbon dioxide to the atmosphere, restarting the entire cycle.
```

### 回看 · T1-15 · 更好版 · 全文
```
The flow chart illustrates the multi-stage process by which ethanol is produced, as well as its subsequent use.

Overall, it is a cyclical process consisting of several steps, beginning with the growth of plants and culminating in the release of carbon dioxide.

The process commences when plants and trees utilize energy from sunlight and carbon dioxide to grow. Once these plants are fully grown, they are harvested by machinery and sent for pre-processing, which converts them into cellulose.

In the following step, the cellulose is further processed to yield sugars, which are subsequently mixed with microbes to produce the final product — ethanol. It can be used for fueling a variety of vehicles — cars, trucks and airplanes. These vehicles give off exhaust fumes, releasing carbon dioxide into the atmosphere and restarting the entire cycle.
```

**⑩ ⛔ 今天大部分 B0 查重是无效的 —— zsh 不做词分割，`dedup $q` 把两个词当成一个（§3.5 第4步 C3 抓到）**
```
现象　`python3 drill.py dedup "scope 范围"` ⇒ **零命中**
　　　`python3 drill.py dedup scope 范围`（两个参数）⇒ 命中 #0030 #0176 #0269 #0408
　　　脚本的横幅会写清楚：前者「查 **1 个词**：scope 范围」· 后者「查 **2 个词**：scope / 范围」
根因　教练今天用 `for q in "a b" "c d"; do drill.py dedup $q; done` 跑批量查重。
　　　**zsh（本机 shell）对无引号变量展开不做词分割**（bash 会），
　　　所以 `$q` 整串被当成**一个查询词** ⇒ 档案里当然没有 "scope 范围" 这个字符串 ⇒ 必然零命中。
影响　今天 34 条新建里，**凡是靠循环跑查重的都拿到了假阴性**（B0 块里写的"零命中"不作数）。
　　　⇒ 本次 §3.5 第 4 步 C3 已经**逐条重跑**（每个关键词单独一个参数），结果见下。

**重跑结果（21 个关键词，看有没有【非今日】的旧号排在前面）**
　scope ⇒ **#0030 #0176 #0269（旧号！）** —— 唯一一处真发现，处理见下
　其余 20 个（mass production／non-disclosure／deform／track／trace／monitor／inherently／waste／
　nineties／specially／especially／subscribe／feasible／viable／combat／tackle／pellets／scraps／
　mineral／law／immigration）排在前面的**要么是今天新建的那条本身**，
　要么是**今天的题面／答案落进了别的条目的历史行**（#0353 #0327 #0190 #0113 #0075 #0368 #0377 #0356…）
　⇒ 逐条看过，**没有一条构成合并**。

**唯一的真发现：#0408（范围＝scope）漏掉了她早就练过的固定块**
```
#0030（🎓 2026-08-18）＝ `out of scope of` ✘ → `**beyond the scope of** a doctor` ✔
⇒ 三问全否（一个管**块的介词与冠词**，一个管**这个名词本身**）⇒ **不合并**，
　 但 #0408 正文必须挂上 beyond the scope of，否则等于把她已有的资产藏起来。
⇒ 已补进 #0408 正文（含与 #0030／#0176 的分工与反向验）。
```

⇒ **判据（本场立，写成动作）**：
　 **跑 `dedup` 一律把每个词写成【独立参数】**：`drill.py dedup scope 范围`，
　 ⛔ 不许写成 `dedup "scope 范围"`、⛔ 不许在 zsh 循环里用无引号的 `$q` 传多词。
　 ★ 自检点：脚本横幅会打印「查 N 个词」——**N 必须等于你想查的词数**，不等就是查错了。

## 收尾（§4⑥ / §8⑤）

### 第 1 件 · 新建条目复查（§3.5 第 4 步）
```
C1 今天新建 **34 条**：#0379 ＋ #0381–#0413（**#0380 撤销未建号**，F07 末尾留墓碑）
   族分布：F01 2（#0383 #0404）· F03 1（#0387）· F04 1（#0409）· F07 1（#0379）
           · F08 **28** · F09 1（#0400）
C2 两两互查：34 条彼此**没有一对是同一条规则** —— 21 条是各自独立的固定块（词组档），
   13 条是各自独立的规则／词表；逐对看过，⛔ 无合并。
C3 每条对全档重跑 dedup —— ⚠️ **这一步抓到了今天最大的方法问题**（见教练侧 ⑩）：
   今天的批量查重因 zsh 不做词分割而**全部假阴性**，本步逐条重跑（每词一个独立参数）。
   重跑 21 个关键词，只有 **scope** 浮出旧号（#0030 #0176 #0269）⇒ 逐条过三问 ⇒ **不合并**，
   但 #0408 正文补挂 `beyond the scope of`（她 08-18 就练过的块）。
C4 合并：**0 条**
C5 「归入待确认」：今天没有打这个标记的条目 ⇒ 无待结清
C6 ⇒ **今天新建 34 条 → 复查后合并 0 条 → 净新增 34 条**
```

### 第 2 件 · `drill.py migrate` 双向搬迁
```
⛔ 第一次跑被**自校拦下并整批回滚**：`problems.md F08 段内编号不是升序`
   —— 教练把 #0402 插到了 #0401 前面（今天挪 F08 块时留下的）。
   ⇒ 按锚点把两块换回升序（#0401 → #0402 → #0403），`check --changed` ERROR 0 后重跑。
★ 这次回滚**证明了这个闸不是摆设**：它不光搬，还验条目正文逐字节、状态字段、check 报告、族内升序。

搬迁结果
  problems.md → graduated.md   **63 条**（今天毕业的全部）
  graduated.md → problems.md    **1 条**（#0248，🎓 后复发，08-31 起就等着搬回）
  problems.md   10577 行 → 6072 行（-4505）
  graduated.md  11612 行 → 16123 行（+4511）
  git --numstat  graduated.md +4631 −90 ｜ problems.md +2115 −4302
  复跑一次：「两边都没有要搬的」⇒ **搬干净了** ✔
  `stats` 里那两条 ⏸ 提示已消失 ✔
```

### ⏸ 等她的（⛔ 不催）
```
① **#0051** 的裁定 —— 标题本身写着"这是个检查动作不是表达"。按她 08-31 的原则，
   要么改写成"写的当下就生效"的形状，要么按 §2⑤ 挂 R 项。今天第三次调出，仍未裁。
② **手滑豁免要不要扩到"相邻两词顺序颠倒"** —— 今天 `cut more once` 那处教练按扩张处理并留痕，
   §3.2 原文写的是"字母顺序颠倒"。
③ **交付物"发全"怎么守**（她 09-01 明确：先不写 skill，收尾后再讨论）。已知事实两条：
   · `deliver` 验的是 session 文件，**看不到聊天**（组3 组7 两次挑着发，闸都报 ERROR 0）
   · `deliver` 只认第一个 `## 回看` 节 ⇒ 复习日两篇新题时，第二篇不进闸
④ 要不要让 `used` 顺手把当天实际用的中文题面写回条目（现在它只记流水）
⑤ 要不要让 `pick` 把"今天已经判过"的条目排到候选池最后（今天 56 条候选里 44 条当天已有读数）
```
